<script>\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
                    const trendData = {\n
                labels: ["31 Aug","01 Sep","02 Sep","03 Sep","04 Sep","07 Sep","08 Sep","09 Sep","10 Sep","11 Sep","15 Sep","16 Sep","17 Sep","18 Sep","21 Sep","22 Sep","23 Sep","24 Sep","25 Sep"],\n
                modalPrices: [80599,80729,83599,79739,89000,65699,83996,85339,81229,78509,57099,70169,67359,60609,74299,83099,83099,80539,60009],\n
                minPrices: [58019,55699,64699,56099,61399,56009,52209,55799,57009,55399,56099,54009,50009,53309,61029,53999,55299,55199,58009],\n
                maxPrices: [93339,98129,88129,87009,92299,72169,85099,93696,88229,87009,75230,84109,83099,75609,76009,94896,88299,98496,80000],\n
                arrivals: [3,17,14,17,15,1,17,8,3,12,1,1,18,1,8,1,4,7,1],\n
                locale: 'kn',\n
            };\n
            let attempts = 0;\n
            const initTrend = () => {\n
                if (typeof window.initPriceTrendChart === 'function') {\n
                    window.initPriceTrendChart('priceTrendCanvas', trendData);\n
                } else if (attempts < 10) {\n
                    attempts++;\n
                    setTimeout(initTrend, 100);\n
                }\n
            };\n
            initTrend();\n
        \n
                    const barFills = document.querySelectorAll('.season-bar-fill');\n
            if (barFills.length > 0) {\n
                const animateBars = () => {\n
                    // Double rAF forces browser to paint height:0 before transitioning\n
                    requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n
                        requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n
                            barFills.forEach((bar, idx) => {\n
                                const targetHeight = bar.getAttribute('data-target-height') || '0%';\n
                                setTimeout(() => {\n
                                    bar.style.height = targetHeight;\n
                                    // After spring-bounce settles (~900ms), activate peak glow + crown + price\n
                                    if (bar.classList.contains('pk')) {\n
                                        setTimeout(() => {\n
                                            bar.classList.add('khc-loaded');\n
                                            const col = bar.closest('.khc-col');\n
                                            if (col) {\n
                                                const crown = col.querySelector('.khc-crown');\n
                                                if (crown) crown.classList.add('khc-loaded');\n
                                                const priceLabel = col.querySelector('.khc-price-inline');\n
                                                if (priceLabel) priceLabel.classList.add('khc-loaded');\n
                                            }\n
                                        }, 900);\n
                                    } else if (bar.classList.contains('lo')) {\n
                                        setTimeout(() => {\n
                                            bar.classList.add('khc-loaded');\n
                                        }, 900);\n
                                    }\n
                                }, 60 * idx);\n
                            });\n
                        });\n
                    });\n
                };\n
\n
                if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {\n
                    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {\n
                        entries.forEach(entry => {\n
                            if (entry.isIntersecting) {\n
                                animateBars();\n
                                observer.disconnect();\n
                            }\n
                        });\n
                    }, { threshold: 0.08 });\n
\n
                    const container = document.querySelector('.season-bars-container');\n
                    if (container) {\n
                        observer.observe(container);\n
                    } else {\n
                        setTimeout(animateBars, 200);\n
                    }\n
                } else {\n
                    setTimeout(animateBars, 200);\n
                }\n
            }\n
            });\n
</script>\n
    </main>\n

Kannada","name_kn":"\u0c89\u0ca4\u0ccd\u0ca4\u0cb0 \u0c95\u0ca8\u0ccd\u0ca8\u0ca1","lat":14.7937,"lon":74.6869},{"id":30,"name":"Vijayanagara","name_kn":"\u0cb5\u0cbf\u0c9c\u0caf\u0ca8\u0c97\u0cb0","lat":15.2689,"lon":76.3909},{"id":31,"name":"Vijayapura","name_kn":"\u0cb5\u0cbf\u0c9c\u0caf\u0caa\u0cc1\u0cb0","lat":16.8302,"lon":75.71},{"id":248,"name":"Yadgir","name_kn":"\u0caf\u0cbe\u0ca6\u0c97\u0cbf\u0cb0\u0cbf","lat":16.77,"lon":77.13}],\n
\n
        get filteredDistricts() {\n
            if (!this.searchFilter.trim()) {\n
                return this.districts;\n
            }\n
            const q = this.searchFilter.toLowerCase().trim();\n
            return this.districts.filter(d => \n
                (d.name && d.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) || \n
                (d.name_kn && d.name_kn.toLowerCase().includes(q))\n
            );\n
        },\n
\n
        openModal() {\n
            this.isOpen = true;\n
            this.isDropdownOpen = true;\n
            this.isUpdating = false;\n
            this.updatingDistrictId = null;\n
            this.searchFilter = '';\n
            this.gpsError = null;\n
            // Preserves website scrollbar - no overflow-hidden on body\n
        },\n
\n
        closeModal() {\n
            if (this.isUpdating) return;\n
            this.isOpen = false;\n
            this.isDropdownOpen = false;\n
        },\n
\n
        toggleDropdown() {\n
            if (this.isUpdating) return;\n
            this.isDropdownOpen = !this.isDropdownOpen;\n
            if (this.isDropdownOpen) {\n
                this.searchFilter = '';\n
                this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        if (this.$refs.dropdownSearch) {\n
                            this.$refs.dropdownSearch.focus();\n
                        }\n
                    }, 100);\n
                });\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        selectDistrictByName(name) {\n
            if (this.isUpdating) return;\n
            const target = this.districts.find(d => d.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());\n
            if (target) {\n
                this.selectDistrict(target);\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        selectDistrict(dist) {\n
            if (this.isUpdating) return;\n
            this.isUpdating = true;\n
            this.updatingDistrictId = dist.id;\n
            this.updatingDistrictName = dist.name;\n
            this.updatingDistrictNameKn = dist.name_kn || dist.name;\n
            this.activeDistrictId = dist.id;\n
            this.activeDistrictName = dist.name;\n
            this.activeDistrictNameKn = dist.name_kn || dist.name;\n
            this.saveAndRedirect(dist.id, dist.name, dist.lat, dist.lon);\n
        },\n
\n
        detectGPSLocation() {\n
            if (this.isUpdating) return;\n
            if (!navigator.geolocation) {\n
                this.gpsError = this.locale === 'en'\n
                    ? 'Your browser does not support GPS. Please select your district from the list below.'\n
                    : 'ನಿಮ್ಮ ಬ್ರೌಸರ್ ಜಿಪಿಎಸ್ ಬೆಂಬಲಿಸುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. ದಯವಿಟ್ಟು ಜಿಲ್ಲಾ ಪಟ್ಟಿಯಿಂದ ಆಯ್ಕೆಮಾಡಿ.';\n
                return;\n
            }\n
\n
            this.isDetecting = true;\n
            this.detectingMessage = this.locale === 'en' ? 'Detecting location...' : 'ಸ್ಥಳ ಪತ್ತೆಹಚ್ಚಲಾಗುತ್ತಿದೆ...';\n
            this.gpsError = null;\n
\n
            navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(\n
                (position) => {\n
                    const lat = position.coords.latitude;\n
                    const lon = position.coords.longitude;\n
                    this.detectingMessage = this.locale === 'en' ? 'Matching nearest Karnataka mandi...' : 'ಸಮೀಪದ ಜಿಲ್ಲೆ ಹೊಂದಿಸಲಾಗುತ್ತಿದೆ...';\n
                    this.findNearestDistrictAndSelect(lat, lon);\n
                },\n
                (error) => {\n
                    console.warn('GPS failed or timed out, trying IP geolocation fallback...', error);\n
                    this.fallbackToIP();\n
                },\n
                {\n
                    enableHighAccuracy: true,\n
                    timeout: 7000,\n
                    maximumAge: 60000\n
                }\n
            );\n
        },\n
\n
        fallbackToIP() {\n
            fetch('https://ipapi.co/json/')\n
                .then(res => res.json())\n
                .then(data => {\n
                    if (data && data.latitude && data.longitude) {\n
                        this.findNearestDistrictAndSelect(data.latitude, data.longitude);\n
                    } else {\n
                        throw new Error('IP coordinates unavailable');\n
                    }\n
                })\n
                .catch(() => {\n
                    this.isDetecting = false;\n
                    this.gpsError = this.locale === 'en'\n
                        ? 'Could not detect location. Please select your district from the list.'\n
                        : 'ಸ್ಥಳ ಪತ್ತೆಹಚ್ಚಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾಗಲಿಲ್ಲ. ದಯವಿಟ್ಟು ಪಟ್ಟಿಯಿಂದ ನಿಮ್ಮ ಜಿಲ್ಲೆಯನ್ನು ಆರಿಸಿ.';\n
                });\n
        },\n
\n
        findNearestDistrictAndSelect(userLat, userLon) {\n
            let nearest = null;\n
            let minDistanceKm = Infinity;\n
\n
            for (const d of this.districts) {\n
                if (d.lat && d.lon) {\n
                    const distKm = this.getHaversineKm(userLat, userLon, d.lat, d.lon);\n
                    if (distKm < minDistanceKm) {\n
                        minDistanceKm = distKm;\n
                        nearest = d;\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            }\n
\n
            if (nearest) {\n
                this.isUpdating = true;\n
                this.updatingDistrictId = nearest.id;\n
                this.updatingDistrictName = nearest.name;\n
                this.updatingDistrictNameKn = nearest.name_kn || nearest.name;\n
                this.saveAndRedirect(nearest.id, nearest.name, userLat, userLon);\n
            } else {\n
                const fallback = this.districts[0] || { id: 1, name: 'Shivamogga' };\n
                this.isUpdating = true;\n
                this.updatingDistrictId = fallback.id;\n
                this.updatingDistrictName = fallback.name;\n
                this.updatingDistrictNameKn = fallback.name_kn || fallback.name;\n
                this.saveAndRedirect(fallback.id, fallback.name);\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        getHaversineKm(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) {\n
            const R = 6371; // Earth radius in km\n
            const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180;\n
            const dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180;\n
            const a = Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +\n
                      Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *\n
                      Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2);\n
            return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));\n
        },\n
\n
        saveAndRedirect(districtId, districtName, lat = null, lon = null) {\n
            // Persist in cookie (1 year)\n
            document.cookie = "selected_district_id=" + districtId + "; path=/; max-age=31536000; SameSite=Lax";\n
            if (window.localStorage) {\n
                localStorage.setItem('krushi_district_id', districtId);\n
                localStorage.setItem('krushi_district_name', districtName);\n
            }\n
\n
            const payload = { district_id: districtId };\n
            if (lat && lon) {\n
                payload.latitude = lat;\n
                payload.longitude = lon;\n
            }\n
\n
            // Post to backend to persist in session\n
            const csrfMeta = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]');\n
            const apiPromise = fetch('http://localhost:8000/set-location', {\n
                method: 'POST',\n
                headers: {\n
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                    'Accept': 'application/json',\n
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfMeta ? csrfMeta.getAttribute('content') : ''\n
                },\n
                body: JSON.stringify(payload)\n
            }).catch(() => {\n
                // Ignore failure, cookie is already saved\n
            });\n
\n
            // Minimum transition delay (500ms) to provide a smooth, delightful visual confirmation\n
            const minDelay = new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));\n
\n
            Promise.all([apiPromise, minDelay]).finally(() => {\n
                // Navigate or reload\n
                const url = new URL(window.location.href);\n
                url.searchParams.set('district', districtId);\n
                window.location.href = url.toString();\n
            });\n
        }\n
    };\n
}\n
</script>\n
\n
    <!-- PWA Install Banner Component -->\n
    <div x-data="{\n
            showInstallPrompt: false,\n
            deferredPrompt: null,\n
            init() {\n
                window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.deferredPrompt = e;\n
                    if (!localStorage.getItem('pwa_prompt_dismissed')) {\n
                        this.showInstallPrompt = true;\n
                    }\n
                });\n
                window.addEventListener('appinstalled', () => {\n
                    this.showInstallPrompt = false;\n
                    this.deferredPrompt = null;\n
                });\n
                window.addEventListener('open-install-prompt', () => {\n
                    this.installApp();\n
                });\n
            },\n
            async installApp() {\n
                if (this.deferredPrompt) {\n
                    this.deferredPrompt.prompt();\n
                    const { outcome } = await this.deferredPrompt.userChoice;\n
                    if (outcome === 'accepted') {\n
                        this.showInstallPrompt = false;\n
                    }\n
                    this.deferredPrompt = null;\n
                } else {\n
                    alert('ನಿಮ್ಮ ಬ್ರೌಸರ್ ಮೆನುವಿನಲ್ಲಿ (Three dots / Share) Add to Home screen ಅಥವಾ Install ಆಯ್ಕೆಮಾಡಿ.');\n
                }\n
            },\n
            dismiss() {\n
                this.showInstallPrompt = false;\n
                localStorage.setItem('pwa_prompt_dismissed', '1');\n
            }\n
        }"\n
        x-show="showInstallPrompt"\n
        x-cloak\n
        x-transition:enter="transition ease-out duration-300 transform"\n
        x-transition:enter-start="translate-y-full opacity-0"\n

    >\n

            <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-white flex items-center justify-center p-1 shrink-0 shadow-inner border border-emerald-400/50 overflow-hidden">\n
                <img src="http://localhost:8000/uploads/branding/app_logo_1790493250.png?v=1790493250" alt="App Icon" class="w-full h-full object-contain">\n
            </div>\n
            <div>\n
                <h4 class="font-bold text-sm text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">\n
                    <span>Install Krushi Baandhava (ಕೃಷಿ ಬಾಂಧವ ಆ್ಯಪ್)</span>\n

                ಇನ್‌ಸ್ಟಾಲ್\n

    <!-- Global Smooth Navigation Feedback & Progress Controller -->\n
    <script>\n
        (function() {\n
            let bar = null;\n
            let timer = null;\n
\n
            function getBar() {\n
                if (!bar) bar = document.getElementById('globalPageProgressBar');\n
                return bar;\n
            }\n
\n
            function startProgress() {\n
                const b = getBar();\n
                if (!b) return;\n
                clearTimeout(timer);\n
                b.style.transition = 'none';\n
                b.style.width = '0%';\n
                b.style.opacity = '1';\n
                requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n
                    b.style.transition = 'width 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.1, 0.85, 0.25, 1)';\n
                    b.style.width = '75%';\n
                });\n
            }\n
\n
            function completeProgress() {\n
                const b = getBar();\n
                if (!b) return;\n
                b.style.transition = 'width 0.15s ease, opacity 0.2s ease 0.1s';\n
                b.style.width = '100%';\n
                b.style.opacity = '0';\n
                timer = setTimeout(() => {\n
                    b.style.width = '0%';\n
                }, 350);\n
            }\n
\n
            // Intercept internal link taps for instant tactile visual feedback\n
            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n
                const link = e.target.closest('a');\n
                if (!link) return;\n
                const href = link.getAttribute('href');\n
                if (!href) return;\n
\n
                // Ignore anchors, external, protocols, new tabs, or keyboard modifiers\n
                if (href.startsWith('#') ||\n
                    href.startsWith('javascript:') ||\n
                    href.startsWith('tel:') ||\n
                    href.startsWith('mailto:') ||\n
                    href.startsWith('whatsapp:') ||\n
                    link.getAttribute('target') === '_blank' ||\n
                    link.hasAttribute('download') ||\n
                    e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey) {\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                try {\n
                    const targetUrl = new URL(link.href, window.location.origin);\n
                    if (targetUrl.origin === window.location.origin) {\n
                        // Skip if already on the exact same page\n
                        if (targetUrl.pathname === window.location.pathname && targetUrl.search === window.location.search) {\n
                            return;\n
                        }\n
                        // Same origin internal page navigation\n
                        startProgress();\n
                    }\n
                } catch(err) {}\n
            }, { passive: true });\n
\n
            // Back/forward cache and unload events\n
            window.addEventListener('pageshow', completeProgress);\n
            window.addEventListener('beforeunload', startProgress);\n
\n
            // Finish any lingering progress upon fresh page load\n
            if (document.readyState === 'complete') {\n
                completeProgress();\n
            } else {\n
                window.addEventListener('load', completeProgress);\n
            }\n
        })();\n
    </script>\n
